Implementation of smooth objective grad:
```
def smooth_obj_grad(theta, beta, n, fsize, mode):
    z = theta - theta.max()
    # maybe clip z to [-50, 60]
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 60) # but clipping max? If clip both, sum okay.
    ez = np.exp(z)
    p = ez / ez.sum()
    c = conv(p) # length 2n-1
    r = 2.0*n*c
    # max
    m = r.max()
    e = np.exp(beta*(r-m))
    total = e.sum()
    if total==0: w=1/(2n-1)
    else w=e/total
    # If beta low? ok.
    # grad_r_p
    W=fft(w,fsize); P=fft(p,fsize)
    grad_p = 4.0*n*real(ifft(W*np.conj(P)))[:n]
    # grad_theta = p*(grad_p - p.dot(grad_p))
    gp_dot = np.dot(p, grad_p)
    grad_theta = p*(grad_p - gp_dot)
    # objective value for minimization: use maybe (1/beta) log total + m? But for L-BFGS, any smooth scaling. Use (m + log total)/beta if beta>0 else m.
    val = (m + np.log(total))/beta
    if mode==0: return val
    else: return val, grad_theta
```

Implementation of `smooth_obj_grad_tuple(theta, beta, n, fsize)`:
```
def smooth_freq(theta, beta, n, fsize):
    z = theta - np.max(theta)
    # clamp z to prevent underflow? if min < -700, exp zero; okay but gradient zero. To maintain positivity, clip at -700 (within bound 100 so not needed). Since θ bounds ±100, z min -200, safe.
    ez = np.exp(z)
    p = ez / np.sum(ez)
    P = fft(p, fsize)
    c = real(ifft(P * P))
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    r = 2.0 * n * c
    m = np.max(r)
    # For beta, if beta <=0 return max? but we use >0
    zr = beta * (r - m)
    # zr <=0, maybe very negative; exp handles
    e = np.exp(zr)
    total = e.sum()
    if total ==0: w = np.full(len(r), 1/len(r))
    else: w = e / total
    val = (m + np.log(total)) / beta
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    grad_p = 4.0 * n * real(ifft(W * np.conj(P)))[:n]
    # Due to numerical, small imaginary ignored.
    mean = np.dot(p, grad_p)
    grad_theta = p * (grad_p - mean)
    return val, grad_theta
```
Need include factor? For φ=logsumexp? We set val=(m+log total)/beta. derivative of val wrt p: (1/β)*∑ β w ∇r =∑w∇r. Our grad_r = 4n crosscorr. Correct. If val scaled by 1/beta, gradient not scaled. Wait derivative of (1/β) log total where total=∑e^{βr}: ∇ = (1/β)*∑ e^{βr} β ∇r / total =∑w∇r. No 1/β. Good.

Smooth val ~2. Gradient of φ at uniform? weights center, grad_p ~? For k=n-1 weight, grad_p[j]=4n p_{k-j} ~4n*1/n=4 for indices? grad_theta ~p*(4 - mean). mean ~? grad ~1e-4 maybe. L-BFGS step maybe ~1. Good? Need maybe gradient magnitude small (~1e-4), L-BFGS default should handle. Could scale objective? If val ~1.5, grad_theta ~1e-4, Hessian maybe 1e-4. Step size initial ~? def exact_objective(p,n):
    fsize=...
    P=fft(p,fsize)
    c=real(ifft(P*P))[:2*n-1]
    return float(2*n*c.max())

Let's write `run_lbfgs(theta0, beta, n, fsize, time_limit, t_eval, rng=None)`:
```
def run_lbfgs(theta0, beta, n, fsize, time_limit, t_eval, maxiter_cap=1000):
    maxfun = int(max(200, time_limit / max(t_eval*1.6, 1e-4)))
    if maxfun > 200000: maxfun=200000
    bounds = [(-100.0, 100.0)] for _ in range(n)] # maybe memory.
    res = optimize.minimize(smooth_objective_tuple, theta0.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,n,fsize), bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': min(2000, maxfun//2), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':30, 'disp':False})
    return res.x, res.fun, res.nit, res.status
```

```
def line_search_projected(p, grad, f, n, fsize, max_tries=8):
    p0=p; f0=f
    step = 1.0/(10.0*n) # maybe
    for try:
        q = p - step*grad
        q = project_simplex(q, 1.0)
        fnew=exact_obj(q)
        # maybe require fnew < f0 - 1e-10
        if fnew < f0 - 1e-10 or step < 1e-10: return q,fnew,step
        step *=0.5
    return p0,f0,0
```

Let's design helper functions in detail:

1. `project_simplex(v, S=1.0)`: Efficient. For n large, sort O(n log n). We may use if fallback. Implementation standard. Handle all negative.
```
def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0: return v
    v = np.asarray(v, float)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1,n+1)
    theta = (cum - S)/idx
    valid = theta <= w
    rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])+1) if np.any(valid) else n
    theta = (cum[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta, 0.0)
```
Need idx float to avoid int division? In Python3 float anyway.

2. `next_fft_size(length)`: `1 << (length -1).bit_length()` maybe if length power of two? For length=1, works. Use `np.int64`.

3. `fft_full_conv(a)`: returns c length 2n-1. Use global? pass fsize.
```
def convolve_self(a, fsize):
    P = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P))
    return c[:2*len(a)-1]
```

4. `exact_objective_p(p, n, fsize)`: maybe returns objective and c? To avoid recompute, return value.

def objective_with_conv(p,n,fsize):
   P=fft(p,fsize); c=real(ifft(P*P))[:2*n-1]; f=2*n*c.max(); return f,c
```

5. `softmax_probs(theta, lower=-700?)`:
```
def softmax_probs(theta):
   z = theta - np.max(theta)
   ez = np.exp(z)
   s = ez.sum()
   if s == 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       # fallback uniform
       return np.full(len(theta), 1.0/len(theta))
   return ez/s
```
Since theta bounds ±100, okay. If max theta inf? no.

p = np.asarray(p,float); p = np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 uniform; p=p/s; p=np.maximum(p, 1e-300/p?); p=p/(p.sum()); theta=np.log(p); return np.clip(theta, lo, hi)
```
If p has zeros, log 1e-300=-690 clipped to -100. That puts mass e^-100; okay. But if many zeros, adding 1e-100 each may alter sum? 8192*1e-44 negligible. Use floor=1e-18? If some zero but many, adding 1e-8? Could distort. Use floor = max(1e-300, 1e-12/n) maybe 1e-16 for n=8192? 1e-12/n=1e-16, log -36, not too low. This adds up to 1e-12 total mass if all? 7. Smooth objective tuple function. Need top-level; uses `fft` `ifft` imported. Also maybe `np`.
```
def smooth_objective_tuple(theta, beta, n, fsize):
    p = softmax_probs(theta)
    P = fft(p, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P))[:2*n-1]
    r = 2.0 * n * c
    m = float(np.max(r))
    zr = beta * (r - m)
    zr = np.minimum(zr, 0.0)  # safety
    e = np.exp(zr)
    total = e.sum()
    if total <= 0.0:
       w = np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
    else:
       w = e / total
    val = (m + np.log(total)) / beta
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    grad_p = 4.0 * n * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(P)))[:n]
    # remove small negative? no
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    grad_theta = p * (grad_p - mean_grad)
    # Clean nonfinite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
       grad_theta = np.nan_to_num(grad_theta)
    return float(val), grad_theta
```
Potential log(total): if total=0 no. If beta small, val? okay. If beta=0? we won't.

Could reduce by using `r` later; if beta high, weights almost uniform over max peaks if many equal. Good.

8. Fixed weight objective tuple:
```
def fixed_objective_tuple(theta, weights, n, fsize):
    p = softmax_probs(theta)
    P = fft(p,fsize)
    c = real(ifft(P*P))[:2*n-1]
    r = 2.0*n*c
    val = float(np.dot(weights, r))
    # weights sum1; val scale ~1.5
    W=fft(weights,fsize)
    grad_p = 4.0*n*real(ifft(W*np.conj(P)))[:n]
    mean = dot(p, grad_p)
    grad = p*(grad_p-mean)
    return val, grad
```
If weights sparse, W FFT each eval; okay. We can optimize if `len(weights)` big? length 2n-1.

9. `lbfgs_minimize_fun(fun, theta0, fun_args, n, time_limit, t_eval, maxfun_cap=50000, ftol=1e-12, gtol=1e-8, upper_bound=100.0)`: calls scipy. Uses `fun(x, args...)`? Since args is tuple. We need pass `args` to minimize. For smooth fun, args=(beta,n,fsize). For fixed, args=(weights,n,fsize). But `minimize` with jac=True and args: fun signature `fun(x, *args)`. Good.
```
def run_fixed_lbfgs(fun, theta0, args, n, time_limit, t_eval, maxfun_cap=50000):
    if time_limit < 5: return theta0.copy(), None
    t_per = max(t_eval, 1e-5)
    maxfun = int(time_limit / (t_per * 1.25))
    if maxfun < 100: maxfun=100
    if maxfun > maxfun_cap: maxfun=maxfun_cap
    maxiter = min(2000, max(fun?)) maybe maxfun//2
    bounds = [(-100.0, 100.0)]*n
    try:
       res = scipy.optimize.minimize(fun, theta0.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=args, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':30})
       if res.success or res.status in (0,1,2,3,4,5,6): return res.x, res.fun
    except Exception as e: print...
    return theta0, None
```

def direct_descend(p, n, fsize, time_limit, rng):

Implement `direct_descent(p,n,fsize,time_limit,rng,beta=200, step_init=None)`:
```
p=project_simplex(p,1.0)
c,r,f = obj_conv
best=f
it=0
step=0.25/n
no_improve=0
while time_left>2:
  r=2n*c
  m=r.max()
  z=beta*(r-m); w=exp(z); total=w.sum(); w/=total
  P=fft(p); W=fft(w); grad=4n*real(ifft(W*conj(P)))[:n]
  # adjust step based on previous? if no_improve increase? 
  accepted=False
  for ls in range(6):
     q=p - step*grad
     q=project_simplex(q,1.0)
     fnew,cnew=exact
     if fnew < f - 1e-12:
        p=q; f=fnew; c=cnew; step*=1.25; accepted=True; break
     step*=0.5
     # if step < 1e-8 break
  if accepted and f<best: best=f; no_improve=0
  else:
     no_improve+=1; step*=0.5
     if no_improve>50:
        # add noise and beta increase
        p=project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0,0.002/n,n),1.0); f,c=exact; no_improve=0; beta*=2? but beta cap
     if no_improve>100: break
  it+=1
return p,best
```
But step maybe too large/small. We can scale by gradient norm: `gmax=np.max(np.abs(grad)); step=0.1/(n*gmax)`? For gmax~4, step~0.025/n =3e-6, too small? Change step*g~1e-5, okay. Let's use `step = 1.0 / (n * max(1.0, np.linalg.norm(grad)/np.sqrt(n)))`? Root mean ~? Simpler: `step = 1.0 / (8.0*n)` (~1.5e-5) and adjust. If no improvement, halve after 5. If many improvements, can increase up to `5/(n)`. Good.

But projected gradient on nonconvex may not find large improvements. Could still help.

`run_lbfgs` signature:
```
def run_lbfgs(fun, theta0, args, n, time_limit, t_eval, maxfun_cap, bounds):
    if time_limit < 3: return theta0.copy(), None
    t_per = max(t_eval, 1e-6)
    maxfun = int(time_limit / (t_per * 1.4))
    maxfun = max(100, min(maxfun, maxfun_cap, 200000))
    maxiter = max(20, min(max(100, maxfun//3), 2000))
    try:
       res = minimize(fun, theta0.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=args, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':30})
       theta=res.x
       # sanitize
       theta=np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0, posinf=100, neginf=-100)
       return theta, res.fun
    except Exception as ex:
       print('lbfgs error', ex)
       return theta0.copy(), None
```
Note `np.nan_to_num` `posinf` available in numpy. Good.

def optimize_one(p0, n, fsize, budget, t_eval, bounds, rng):
    p = normalize(p0)
    f = exact
    best_p=p.copy(); best_f=f
    theta=theta_from_probs(p); theta_best=theta.copy()
    start=time.monotonic()
    stages=[(1.0,0.06,3000),(3.0,0.10,5000),(10.0,0.18,8000),(50.0,0.24,10000),(200.0,0.22,10000),(800.0,0.20,8000)]
    # Adjust based on budget: if budget<200 maybe fewer.
    for beta, frac, cap in stages:
       remaining = budget - (time.monotonic()-start)
       if remaining < 20: break
       alloc = frac*budget
       # maybe cap alloc by remaining
       theta2, val = run_lbfgs(smooth_objective_tuple, theta, (beta,n,fsize), n, alloc, t_eval, cap, bounds)
       p2=softmax_probs(theta2)
       f2=exact
       if f2 < best_f:
           best_f=f2; best_p=p2.copy(); theta_best=theta2.copy()
       if f2 <= best_f * 1.02:
           theta=theta2.copy()
       else:
           # if we have a good best, revert; else keep
           if best_f < np.inf and f2 > best_f*1.05:
               theta=theta_best.copy()
           else:
               theta=theta2.copy()
       print progress
    # active polish if remaining? do inside propose
    return best_p,best_f
```

```
def active_weight_polish(p, n, fsize, time_limit, t_eval, bounds, rng):
    start=time.monotonic()
    p = project_simplex(p,1.0).astype
    theta=theta_from_probs(p); best_p=p; best_f=exact_objective_p(p,n,fsize)
    outer=0
    no_improve=0
    alphas_w=[20,50,100]
    T_caps=[64,128,256,512]
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit - 5 and no_improve<4:
        remaining = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-start)
        if remaining < 20: break
        c = objective? Need c to compute weights. We'll compute conv and r.
        P=fft(p), c=real(ifft(P*P)); r=2n*c; f=r.max(); if f<best_f update.
        order=np.argsort(r)[::-1]
        # choose K size increasing
        Ksize=min(T_caps[outer % len(T_caps)], 2*n-1)
        K=order[:Ksize]
        # threshold expansion: include all with r >= r.max()-0.01*r.max() capped
        thresh = f - max(0.005*f, 1e-8)
        Km = order[r[order] >= thresh]
        if len(Km) < Ksize: Km = np.concatenate((K, Km))? Keep unique. Simpler: use union cap 1024: take order where idx in set? Avoid set.
        # Could just top Ksize for simplicity.
        # weights: softmax of r[K] with beta_w=20 (or if K small uniform)
        active_r = r[K]
        wact = np.exp(20*(active_r/active_r.max()-1.0)); wact/=wact.sum()
        weights=np.zeros(2*n-1); weights[K]=wact
        chunk = min(30.0, remaining/ max(1, 4-outer?))
        theta2, val = run_lbfgs(fixed_objective_tuple, theta, (weights,n,fsize), n, chunk, t_eval, 4000, bounds)
        p2=softmax_probs(theta2); f2=exact
        if f2 < best_f*(1-1e-10):
            best_f=f2; best_p=p2; theta=theta2; no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            # perturb theta
            theta = theta + rng.normal(0, 0.02, n)
            theta=np.clip(theta, -100,100)
        outer +=1
    return best_p,best_f
```

def direct_descend(p,n,fsize,time_limit,rng,beta_dir=100, max_iter=20000):
   start=...
   p=project_simplex(p,1.0)
   c=_conv(p); f=2n*c.max(); best=p,f
   step=1.0/(8.0*n)
   beta=beta_dir
   no=0
   it=0
   while time<... and it<max_iter:
       r=2n*c
       m=r.max(); z=beta*(r-m); w=exp(z); total=w.sum(); w/=total
       P=fft(p); W=fft(w); grad=4n*real(ifft(W*conj(P)))[:n]
       # scale step if grad typical
       gscale=np.max(np.abs(grad))
       if gscale>0: step_desired=0.25/(n*gscale)
       if step > 10*step_desired: step=step_desired
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(5):
          q=p - step*grad
          q=project_simplex(q,1.0)
          cq=conv(q); fq=2n*cq.max()
          if fq < f - 1e-12:
             p=q; f=fq; c=cq; step*=1.1 (cap); accepted=True; break
          step*=0.5
       if accepted and f<best_f: best... no=0
       else:
          no+=1
          if no>30:
             # noise
             p=project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0, 5e-6, n),1.0); c=conv; f=...
             no=0
          if no>80: break
       it+=1
   return best...
```

```
def quick_project(v,S=1.0):
   v=np.maximum(v,0.0); s=v.sum(); if s<=0: return uniform; return v*(S/s)
```

Detailed function implementations:

`normalize_p(v)` top-level:
```
def normalize_p(v):
    a = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)/n
    return a / s
```

`theta_from_probs(p, floor=None, lo=-100.0, hi=100.0)`:
```
def theta_from_probs(p, lo=-100.0, hi=100.0):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n=p.size
    floor = max(1e-300, 1e-12/float(n))
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()
    theta = np.log(p)
    return np.clip(theta, lo, hi)
```

`softmax_probs(theta)`:
```
def softmax_probs(theta):
    z = theta - np.max(theta)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
    return ez/s
```
Since theta may have values at -100, max maybe 100, z min -200, exp 1e-87, sum okay.

`convolve_self_fixed(a, fsize)`? We need functions returning c, maybe objective.
```
def compute_conv(a, fsize):
    P = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P))
    return c[:2*len(a)-1]
```

def objective_from_p(p,n,fsize):

    f = float(2.0*n*np.max(c))
    return f, c
```

def estimate_eval(theta0,n,fsize,beta=10.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    smooth_objective_tuple(theta0,beta,n,fsize)
    t1=time.monotonic()
    # second call
    smooth_objective_tuple(theta0,beta*2.0,n,fsize)
    t2=time.monotonic()
    dt = max(1e-4, (t1-t0)*0.5 + (t2-t1)*0.5)
    return dt
```

`central_alias`:
```
def central_alias(n, alpha=0.5):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1):
        a[i+1]=a[i]*((i+alpha)/(i+1.0))
    return a
```
Check ratio for i=0: alpha/(1) if alpha=0.5 =>0.5. Good. For alpha arbitrary: coefficient of (1-z)^-α: a_{i+1}/a_i=(i+α)/(i+1). For α=0.5, good.

def make_bg_candidate(c, bg_mult, mode, n):
    base = c.copy()

    b = bg_mult * bmax

        elif mode == 'left': base = base + b*(1.0 - t)
        elif mode == 'lin': base = base + b*(t)
        elif mode == 'rand': need rng? Use parameter?

Actually in `_minimize_lbfgsb` (forwrap) it uses `res = lbfgsb(..., fprime=...)`: the wrapper `LBFGS_B_pyproduct` likely calls `myf` and `myg` together? It has `retval = self._product(...)`? In SciPy's `LBFGS_B_pyfunction`, when `fprime` provided, it calls `fprime(x, *args)` in the `_product` function? Let's recall: It defines `m = lbfgsb.factr` and passes `f` and `g`? It calls `self._fb(x, nmlx)` which calls `self._f` and if `self._g` calls g. It calls f and g in same `__call__`. Then it uses `_update`? In `_line_search`, it calls function `self._fb(x, n)` which computes f and g. So `f` and `g` are called together? It may call f and g separately if not `jac=True`? In `minimize_lbfgsb`, it creates `myf` wrapping fun, and if jac callable sets `_g=jac`. Then in `LBFGS_B_pyfunction._fb`, it calls `myf(x,*args)` and `myg(x,*args)`. So two calls. `compute_conv`:
```
def compute_conv(a, fsize):
    n = len(a)
    size = 2*n - 1
    P = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P))
    return c[:size]
```
If fsize less than size? next_fft_size ensures.

`objective_only`:
```
def objective_only(p, n, fsize):
    c = compute_conv(p, fsize)
    if c.size == 0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
```

`add_bg`: For ramp_up/dn, creates t each call; could be slow but candidates ~100, n=8192, fine. To reduce, in generate we can pass t but top-level function can't nested; can pass t as arg. Let's do `add_bg(c, bm, mode, t)` to avoid linspace. If t None create.

```
def add_bg(c, bm, mode, t=None):
    base=np.array(c, copy=True)
    if bm != 0.0:
       b = bm * float(np.max(c))
       if t is None: t=np.linspace(0,1,len(base),endpoint=False)
       if mode == 'const': base += b
       elif mode == 'ramp_up': base += b*(0.25 + 0.75*t)  # positive
       elif mode == 'ramp_down': base += b*(1.0 - 0.75*t)
       else: base += b
       base = np.maximum(base,0.0)
    return base
```
For bm=0, only const name. For nonzero, modes. Sums vary; okay.

  for bm in bms:
    if bm == 0:
       base=c
       raw.append((name, base))
    else:
       for mode in ['const','ramp_up','ramp_down']:
          base=add_bg(c,bm,mode,t)
          raw.append(...)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The algorithm combines:
- explicit high-quality parametric initializations (generalized central-benchmark
  sequences with background terms, ramps, smooth random spectra);
- local minimization using L-BFGS-B on a softmax simplex parameterization,
  applied to log-sum-exp approximations of the max-convolution objective;
- active-weight quadratic polishing;
- a direct projected-gradient polish.

The objective is scale invariant. Internally we mostly work with probability
sequences (sum 1); the returned sequence is scaled, but the evaluation is the
same.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from operator import itemgetter


def next_fft_size(length):
    length = max(1, int(length))
    return 1 << (length - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_p(v):
    a = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / s


def softmax_probs(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    if theta.size == 0:
        return theta
    z = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(theta.shape, dtype=np.float64) / theta.size
    return ez / s


def theta_from_probs(p, lo=-100.0, hi=100.0):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    floor = max(1e-300, 1e-12 / float(n))
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        p = p / s
    theta = np.log(p)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    return np.clip(theta, lo, hi)


def quick_project(v, S=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(v.shape, dtype=np.float64) * (S / float(v.size))
    return v * (S / s)


def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cum - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else:
        rho = n
    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def compute_conv(a, fsize):
    n = int(len(a))
    size = max(1, 2 * n - 1)
    P = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P))
    return c[:size]


def objective_only(p, n, fsize):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = compute_conv(p, fsize)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c)))


def smooth_objective_tuple(theta, beta, n, fsize):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    p = softmax_probs(theta)
    P = fft(p, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P))[:2 * n - 1]
    r = 2.0 * float(n) * c

    m = float(np.max(r))
    zr = float(beta) * (r - m)
    zr = np.minimum(zr, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(zr)
    total = float(np.sum(e))
    if total <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(total):
        w = np.full(r.shape, 1.0 / r.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = e / total

    val = (m + float(np.log(total))) / float(beta)

    W = fft(w, fsize)
    grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(P)))[:n]
    grad_p = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_p), grad_p, 0.0)
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    grad_theta = p * (grad_p - mean_grad)
    grad_theta = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_theta), grad_theta, 0.0)
    return float(val), grad_theta


def fixed_objective_tuple(theta, weights, n, fsize):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    p = softmax_probs(theta)
    P = fft(p, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P))[:2 * n - 1]
    r = 2.0 * float(n) * c

    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64)
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        wu = np.full(r.shape, 1.0 / r.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        wu = w / sw

    val = float(np.dot(wu, r))
    W = fft(wu, fsize)
    grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(P)))[:n]
    grad_p = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_p), grad_p, 0.0)
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    grad_theta = p * (grad_p - mean_grad)
    grad_theta = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_theta), grad_theta, 0.0)
    return float(val), grad_theta


def run_lbfgs(fun, theta0, args, n, time_limit, t_eval, maxfun_cap, bounds):
    theta0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64)
    if time_limit < 3.0:
        return theta0.copy(), None

    t_per = max(float(t_eval), 1e-5)
    maxfun = int(time_limit / (t_per * 2.0))
    maxfun = max(120, min(maxfun, int(maxfun_cap), 220000))
    maxiter = max(20, min(3000, max fun := 0, maxfun // 2) if False else max(20, min(3000, maxfun // 2)))

    if bounds is None or len(bounds) != n:
        bounds = [(-100.0, 100.0)] * n

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun,
            theta0.copy(),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=args,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 30,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
        theta = np.clip(theta, -100.0, 100.0)
        return theta, float(res.fun) if np.isfinite(res.fun) else None
    except Exception as ex:
        print("  L-BFGS error:", ex)
        return theta0.copy(), None


def estimate_eval_time(theta0, n, fsize):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    smooth_objective_tuple(theta0, 2.0, n, fsize)
    t1 = time.monotonic()
    smooth_objective_tuple(theta0, 10.0, n, fsize)
    t2 = time.monotonic()
    dt = 0.5 * (t1 - t0) + 0.5 * (t2 - t1)
    return max(dt, 1e-4)


def central_alias(n, alpha=0.5):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    alph = float(alpha)
    for i in range(n - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * ((i + alph) / float(i + 1))
    return a


def add_bg(c, bm, mode, t=None):
    base = np.array(c, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if bm == 0.0:
        return base
    b = float(bm) * float(np.max(base))
    if b <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(b):
        return base
    if t is None or len(t) != len(base):
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(base), endpoint=False)
    if mode == "const":
        base += b
    elif mode == "ramp_up":
        base += b * (0.25 + 0.75 * t)
    elif mode == "ramp_down":
        base += b * (1.0 - 0.75 * t)
    else:
        base += b
    base = np.maximum(base, 0.0)
    return base


def generate_candidates(n, parent_p, rng, fsize):
    raw = []

    if parent_p is not None:
        raw.append(("parent", parent_p))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)

    alphas = [0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.70]
    bgs = [0.0, 0.001, 0.003, 0.01, 0.03, 0.10]
    for alpha in alphas:
        c = central_alias(n, alpha)
        for bm in bgs:
            if bm == 0.0:
                name = "central_%.2f" % alpha
                raw.append((name, c))
            else:
                for mode in ("const", "ramp_up", "ramp_down"):
                    name = "central_%.2f_%s_b%.3f" % (alpha, mode, bm)
                    raw.append((name, add_bg(c, bm, mode, t)))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        raw.append(("exp_fwd_%s" % alpha, np.exp(alpha * t)))
        raw.append(("exp_bwd_%s" % alpha, np.exp(alpha * (1.0 - t))))

    for pw in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        raw.append(("pow_fwd_%s" % pw, (t + 0.05) ** pw))
        raw.append(("pow_bwd_%s" % pw, (1.0 - t + 0.05) ** pw))

    c05 = central_alias(n, 0.5)
    sd = next_fft_size(n)
    for k in range(12):
        x = rng.normal(size=n)
        X = fft(x, sd)
        keep = np.zeros(sd, dtype=np.float64)
        keep[:12] = 1.0
        if sd > 24:
            keep[-12:] = 1.0
        X = X * keep
        x = np.real(ifft(X))[:n]
        x = x - float(np.mean(x))
        sx = float(np.std(x))
        if sx > 1e-12:
            x = x / sx
        amp = 0.4 + float(rng.random())
        base = np.exp(amp * x + 0.35 * t)
        raw.append(("rand_%d" % k, base))
        base2 = c05 + 0.02 * (1.0 + np.exp(amp * x + 0.35 * t))
        raw.append(("crend_%d" % k, base2))

    scored = []
    for name, base in raw:
        p = normalize_p(base)
        if p.size != n:
            continue
        f = objective_only(p, n, fsize)
        if np.isfinite(f):
            scored.append((f, p, name))

    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    return scored[:40]


def optimize_one(p0, n, fsize, budget, t_eval, bounds, rng):
    p0 = normalize_p(p0)
    f0 = objective_only(p0, n, fsize)
    if not np.isfinite(f0):
        p0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        f0 = objective_only(p0, n, fsize)

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_f = float(f0)

    theta = theta_from_probs(p0)
    theta_best = theta.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()

    if budget > 600.0:
        stages = [
            (1.0, 0.05, 3000),
            (3.0, 0.10, 6000),
            (10.0, 0.18, 10000),
            (50.0, 0.25, 14000),
            (200.0, 0.20, 10000),
            (800.0, 0.22, 8000),
        ]
    elif budget > 200.0:
        stages = [
            (2.0, 0.15, 5000),
            (10.0, 0.25, 8000),
            (50.0, 0.30, 10000),
            (200.0, 0.30, 8000),
        ]
    else:
        stages = [
            (5.0, 0.40, 5000),
            (100.0, 0.60, 5000),
        ]

    print("    optimizing %d-point sequence, initial=%.6f, budget=%.1fs" %
          (n, best_f, budget))

    for beta, frac, cap in stages:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        remaining = budget - elapsed
        if remaining < 15.0:
            break
        alloc = frac * budget
        alloc = max(5.0, min(alloc, remaining))

        theta_new, val = run_lbfgs(
            smooth_objective_tuple,
            theta,
            (float(beta), int(n), int(fsize)),
            n,
            alloc,
            t_eval,
            int(cap),
            bounds,
        )
        p_new = softmax_probs(theta_new)
        f_new = objective_only(p_new, n, fsize)

        if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < best_f:
            best_f = float(f_new)
            best_p = p_new.copy()
            theta_best = theta_new.copy()

        # Keep the current point unless it is clearly worse than the best.
        if not np.isfinite(f_new):
            theta = theta_best.copy()
        elif f_new <= best_f * 1.02:
            theta = theta_new.copy()
        elif f_new <= best_f * 1.08:
            theta = theta_new.copy()
        else:
            theta = theta_best.copy()

        print("      beta=%7.1f  exact=%.6f" % (beta, best_f))

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
    return best_p, best_f, elapsed


def active_weight_polish(p0, n, fsize, time_limit, t_eval, bounds, rng):
    p0 = normalize_p(p0)
    start = time.monotonic()

    f0 = objective_only(p0, n, fsize)
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_f = float(f0)
    theta = theta_from_probs(best_p)

    Kcaps = [64, 128, 256, 512, 768]
    no_improve = 0
    outer = 0

    print("    active-weight polish, start=%.6f, limit=%.1fs" % (best_f, time_limit))

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        remaining = time_limit - elapsed
        if remaining < 20.0 or outer > 6:
            break

        c = compute_conv(best_p, fsize)
        r = 2.0 * float(n) * c
        fcur = float(np.max(r))
        if fcur < best_f:
            best_f = fcur
            theta = theta_from_probs(best_p)

        order = np.argsort(r)[::-1]
        Ksize = min(Kcaps[outer % len(Kcaps)], max(1, len(r)))
        K = order[:Ksize]

        active_r = r[K]
        armax = float(np.max(active_r))
        if armax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(armax):
            wact = np.full(len(K), 1.0 / max(1, len(K)), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            wact = np.exp(20.0 * (active_r / armax - 1.0))
            sw = float(np.sum(wact))
            if sw <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
                wact = np.full(len(K), 1.0 / max(1, len(K)), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                wact = wact / sw

        weights = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        weights[K] = wact

        chunk = min(35.0, max(8.0, remaining / 3.0))
        theta_new, val = run_lbfgs(
            fixed_objective_tuple,
            theta,
            (weights, int(n), int(fsize)),
            n,
            chunk,
            t_eval,
            6000,
            bounds,
        )
        p_new = softmax_probs(theta_new)
        f_new = objective_only(p_new, n, fsize)

        if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < best_f * (1.0 - 1e-10):
            best_f = float(f_new)
            best_p = p_new.copy()
            theta = theta_new.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            theta = theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -100.0, 100.0)

        outer += 1
        if no_improve >= 4:
            break

    return best_p, best_f


def direct_descend(p0, n, fsize, time_limit, rng):
    p0 = normalize_p(p0)
    start = time.monotonic()
    p = p0.copy()

    c = compute_conv(p, fsize)
    f = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_f = float(f)

    step = 1.0 / (4.0 * float(n))
    beta = 80.0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    print("    direct polish, start=%.6f, limit=%.1fs" % (best_f, time_limit))

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        if elapsed > max(5.0, time_limit - 3.0):
            break
        if it > 60000:
            break
        it += 1

        r = 2.0 * float(n) * c
        m = float(np.max(r))
        zr = float(beta) * (r - m)
        zr = np.minimum(zr, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(zr)
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if sw <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
            w = np.full(r.shape, 1.0 / r.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / sw

        P = fft(p, fsize)
        W = fft(w, fsize)
        grad = 4.0 * float(n) * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(P)))[:n]
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

        gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if gscale > 0 and np.isfinite(gscale):
            preferred = 1.0 / (float(n) * gscale)
            if step > 10.0 * preferred:
                step = preferred

        accepted = False
        try_step = step
        for _ in range(5):
            q = p - try_step * grad
            q = quick_project(q, 1.0)
            cq = compute_conv(q, fsize)
            fq = float(2.0 * n * np.max(cq))
            if np.isfinite(fq) and fq < f - 1e-12:
                p = q
                c = cq
                f = fq
                step = min(5.0 / float(n), step * 1.05)
                accepted = True
                break
            try_step *= 0.5

        if accepted:
            if f < best_f:
                best_f = float(f)
                best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            step *= 0.6
            if no_improve == 40:
                beta = min(2000.0, beta * 1.8)
            if no_improve == 80:
                p = quick_project(p + rng.normal(0.0, 3e-6, n), 1.0)
                c = compute_conv(p, fsize)
                f = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
                no_improve = 0
            if no_improve > 140:
                break

    return best_p, best_f


def clean_final_sequence(arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if arr.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return arr


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget_s - 30.0)

    # Determine n and parent sequence.
    parent_arr = None
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        parent_arr = np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is None or parent_arr.size < 64 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent_arr)):
        parent_arr = None
    else:
        parent_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(parent_arr), parent_arr, 0.0)
        parent_arr = np.maximum(parent_arr, 0.0)
        # Downsample extreme parent lengths to stay practical.
        if parent_arr.size > 16384:
            target = 16384
            pad = (target - (parent_arr.size % target)) % target
            if pad:
                parent_arr = np.concatenate([parent_arr, np.zeros(pad, dtype=np.float64)])
            parent_arr = parent_arr.reshape(-1, target).mean(axis=0)

    if parent_arr is None:
        n = 8192
        parent_p = None
    else:
        n = int(parent_arr.size)
        parent_p = normalize_p(parent_arr)
        max_p = float(np.max(parent_p))
        if n < 64 or not np.isfinite(max_p) or max_p <= 0:
            n = 8192
            parent_p = None

    fsize = next_fft_size(2 * n - 1)
    bounds = [(-100.0, 100.0)] * n

    # Initial candidate pool.
    scored = generate_candidates(n, parent_p, rng, fsize)
    if not scored:
        p = central_alias(n, 0.5)
        p = normalize_p(p)
        f = objective_only(p, n, fsize)
        scored = [(f, p, "central_0.50")]

    best_p, best_f = scored[0][1].copy(), float(scored[0][0])
    if parent_p is not None:
        fp = objective_only(parent_p, n, fsize)
        if np.isfinite(fp) and fp < best_f:
            best_f = fp
            best_p = parent_p.copy()

    # Eval timing.
    theta0 = theta_from_probs(best_p)
    t_eval = estimate_eval_time(theta0, n, fsize)
    print("n=%d fsize=%d t_eval=%.5fs initial_best=%.6f" %
          (n, fsize, t_eval, best_f))

    # Choose a few diverse candidates for full local optimization.
    chosen = []
    chosen_names = set()
    for f, p, name in scored:
        if name not in chosen_names:
            chosen.append((name, p, f))
            chosen_names.add(name)
            if len(chosen) == 4:
                break
    # Force a central-benchmark candidate to be included unless it is already present.
    if not any(name.startswith("central") for name in chosen_names):
        for f, p, name in scored:
            if name.startswith("central"):
                if len(chosen) >= 4:
                    chosen.pop()
                    chosen_names.discard(chosen[-1][0]) if chosen else None
                    if chosen:
                        chosen_names.discard(chosen[-1][0])
                chosen.append((name, p, f))
                chosen_names.add(name)
                break

    # Parent first if available.
    if parent_p is not None:
        parent_budget = min(1000.0, max(120.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.42))
        p_new, f_new, _ = optimize_one(parent_p, n, fsize, parent_budget, t_eval, bounds, rng)
        if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < best_f:
            best_f = float(f_new)
            best_p = p_new.copy()
            print("  parent optimized -> %.6f" % best_f)

    # Alternatives.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    per_alt = min(280.0, max(60.0, remaining * 0.08))
    for name, p_cand, f_cand in chosen:
        if name == "parent" or parent_p is not None and np.allclose(p_cand, normalize_p(parent_p)):
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        p_new, f_new, _ = optimize_one(p_cand, n, fsize, per_alt, t_eval, bounds, rng)
        if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < best_f:
            best_f = float(f_new)
            best_p = p_new.copy()
        print("  candidate %s -> %.6f (global %.6f)" % (name, f_new, best_f))
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 150.0:
            break

    # Active-weight polish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 120.0:
        polish_limit = min(520.0, remaining * 0.45)
        p_new, f_new = active_weight_polish(best_p, n, fsize, polish_limit, t_eval, bounds, rng)
        if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < best_f:
            best_f = float(f_new)
            best_p = p_new.copy()
        print("  after active polish -> %.6f" % best_f)

    # Direct polish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 150.0:
        direct_limit = min(560.0, remaining * 0.55)
        p_new, f_new = direct_descend(best_p, n, fsize, direct_limit, rng)
        if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < best_f:
            best_f = float(f_new)
            best_p = p_new.copy()
        print("  after direct polish -> %.6f" % best_f)

    # Final normalization and sanity fallback.
    best_p = normalize_p(best_p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_p)) or float(np.sum(best_p)) < 0.01:
        best_p = normalize_p(central_alias(n, 0.5))

    # Scale final heights. Scaling does not change the objective.
    scale = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    final_arr = best_p * float(scale)
    final_arr = clean_final_sequence(final_arr)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]

    try:
        verified = evaluate_sequence(final_list)  # noqa: F821
        if not np.isfinite(verified):
            raise ValueError("non-finite verified score")
    except Exception as ex:
        print("  final verification failed, fallback:", ex)
        n = 8192
        p = normalize_p(central_alias(n, 0.5))
        final_arr = clean_final_sequence(p * np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
        final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]
        try:
            verified = evaluate_sequence(final_list)  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            verified = np.inf

    print("Returned length=%d internal=%.6f verified=%s" %
          (len(final_list), best_f, ("%.6f" % verified) if np.isfinite(verified) else "inf"))
    return final_list
```
